*Shashi Kumar,Yanis Labrak,Hasindri Watawana,Sergio Burdisso,Esaú Villatoro-Tello,Kadri Hacioğlu,Petr Motlicek,Andreas Stolcke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出在隐私受限领域（如银行）使用合成语音进行LLM-based ASR适配时，强化学习（GRPO）优于监督微调（SFT），仅用合成语音即可将WER相对降低40%，结合SFT+GRPO可达45%，增益源于行为层面而非表征层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私法规导致真实语音采集成本高且受限，合成TTS语音成为替代，但合成语音与真实录音存在声学失配，现有工作局限于SFT，亟需更有效的适配方法。

Method: 采用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)，一种无需评论家的强化学习方法，以低WER假设为奖励信号，在合成语音上直接优化策略；并对比了SFT、GRPO及SFT-then-GRPO组合。

Result: 仅合成语音下GRPO将WER从36.71%降至22.09%（相对降低40%），SFT-then-GRPO进一步降至约20%（相对降低45%）；分析显示GRPO主要减少插入错误，改善停止校准和注意力锚定，早期层表征保持不变。

Conclusion: 当合成语音为主要资源时，强化学习（GRPO）应优于监督微调用于LLM-based ASR的领域适配，能显著缩小合成-真实域差距。

Abstract: LLM-based ASR adapted to regulated domains such as banking is bottlenecked by privacy: real speech is costly and legally constrained to collect, making synthetic text-to-speech (TTS) an attractive substitute. Yet synthetic speech stays acoustically mismatched with real recordings, and work on this gap has stayed within supervised fine-tuning (SFT). We instead turn to reinforcement learning, and show that Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) extracts far more from the same synthetic speech than SFT. Synthetic-only adaptation of the model with GRPO, a critic-free method rewarding low-WER hypotheses, reduces WER by 40\% relative to SFT (36.71\%$\to$22.09\%), and an SFT-then-GRPO combination pushes this further to 45\%. We trace the gain to behavior rather than representation: GRPO reduces insertion errors by improving stopping calibration and speech-to-text alignment by better anchoring attention to audio, leaving early-layer representations intact. When synthetic speech is the main resource, reinforcement learning should be preferred over supervised fine-tuning.

</details>


### [34] [Detecting Ladder Logic Bombs in IEC 61131-3 PLC Programs using ESBMC-PLC+: A Formal Verification Approach with Trigger Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08417)
*Pierre Dantas,Lucas Cordeiro,Waldir Junior*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 ESBMC-LLB，一种基于语义模型检查的梯形逻辑炸弹检测工具，通过建模函数块逻辑并结合 k-归纳法与有界模型检查，实现了对所有扫描周期的无界正确性证明及触发条件的精确提取，在多个数据集上表现优于现有 CFG 分析方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有梯形图验证器在中间表示中丢弃函数块主体，导致隐藏其中的恶意逻辑（梯形逻辑炸弹）对证明器不可见；且基于控制流图的分析无法应对自适应触发器（计算型、不透明算术、多扫描等）。需要一种能形式化建模完整 PLC 程序、提供无界证明并自动提取触发条件的方法。

Method: 基于 ESBMC-PLC+ 验证引擎，增加建模层暴露函数块内部逻辑；将炸弹检测重述为形式化验证问题：扫描看门狗建模检测非终止载荷，输出接线建模检测执行器伪造载荷为安全性违例；采用 k-归纳法给出跨所有扫描周期的无界无炸弹证明，有界模型检查器返回的反例即为触发条件。

Result: 在公开 Iacobelli 2024 数据集上检出全部 30 个炸弹并恢复所有触发器，同时检出能规避 CFG 分析的自适应触发器。在 PLC-Defuser SWaT 语料库 v1.0.0 上检出 149/150 个炸弹（99%，零误报），均恢复触发器；后续含非线性非终止炸弹的版本因 SMT 求解器超时检出率降至 49%。

Conclusion: 语义模型检查与 CFG 分析互补：前者提供无界证明、自适应触发器鲁棒性，处理布尔/整数及线性模拟逻辑；后者在非线性模拟非终止场景占优。论文明确划定了两者各自的适用边界。

Abstract: A Ladder Logic Bomb (LLB) is malicious control logic in a Programmable Logic Controller (PLC) program that lies dormant until a trigger activates a payload to manipulate actuators, forge sensor readings, or deny operator control. We observe that real malicious logic hides inside function-block bodies, which existing ladder-diagram verifiers drop from their intermediate representation (IR), making bombs invisible to provers. We present ESBMC-LLB, which uses ESBMC-PLC+ as its verification engine and adds a modeling layer that exposes function-block logic and recasts bomb detection as a formal verification problem: a scan-watchdog exposes non-termination payloads, and output wiring exposes actuator-forgery payloads as safety violations. k-induction gives an unbounded proof of bomb-absence across all scans, and the bounded model checker returns a counterexample that is the trigger - guarantees that signature, anomaly, and CFG-triage detectors lack. On the public Iacobelli 2024 dataset, ESBMC-LLB detects all 30 bombs and recovers every trigger; it also detects adaptive triggers (computed, opaque-arithmetic, multi-scan) that evade CFG-triage. We also report the first semantic model-checker evaluation on PLC-Defuser's SWaT corpus: our analog extension makes the full corpus parseable; on v1.0.0, it detects 149/150 bombs (99%) with zero false positives, recovering each trigger; on a later version with nonlinear non-termination bombs, detection drops to 49% as the SMT solver times out. We conclude that semantic model checking and CFG-triage are complementary - the former gives unbounded proofs, adaptive-trigger robustness, and handles Boolean/integer and linear analog logic; the latter leads to nonlinear analog non-termination, and we delineate where each wins.

</details>


### [35] [Two Axes of LLM Abstention: Answer Correctness and Question Answerability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08456)
*Benedikt J. Wagner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出双轴拒绝策略，分离回答正确性与问题可答性，利用隐藏状态探针检测虚假前提，构建校准策略显著提升拒绝精度与覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型仅用单一置信度阈值决定拒绝，无法区分“会答错的问题”与“根本不该回答的问题（如不可答、虚假前提）”，导致拒绝策略失效。

Method: 在五个指令微调模型（2B-14B）上实验，对比普通答案置信度与隐藏状态线性探针，发现二者分别追踪正确性与可答性；构建双阈值校准策略，结合可答性分数与正确性分数独立认证。

Result: 隐藏状态探针在CREPE虚假前提检测上达0.69-0.77 AUROC，远超基线；直接指令模型检查前提反而导致57%误报，探针路由指令使挑战精度约三倍提升；双轴策略在0.75正确答案覆盖下认证双预算，单阈值仅0.31，14B模型仅双轴策略可认证。

Conclusion: 模型内部表征了可答性但不上报，双轴校准策略可有效分离两类拒绝，随规模增大保证收紧，为可靠拒绝提供可扩展方案。

Abstract: A model should refuse two different things: answers it would get wrong, and questions it should not answer at all, such as unanswerable ones or ones resting on a false premise. The usual recipe thresholds a single confidence score, which cannot tell these apart. Across five instruction-tuned models from three families (2B to 14B), we find they are separate axes. Ordinary answer-confidence tracks whether an answer is right but is nearly blind to whether the question is answerable; a linear probe on hidden states does the reverse. The blind spot does not shrink with scale. It is worst on naturally occurring false-premise questions (CREPE). There, answer-confidence, P(IK), P(True), and even asking the model outright whether a premise is false all stay near chance, while a hidden-state probe reaches 0.69 to 0.77 AUROC: the model represents a problem it will not report. This turns out to be fixable. Instructing a model to check premises backfires, because it then disputes sound and false premises alike (57% false challenges), unable to tell them apart; routing the same instruction with the probe roughly triples challenge precision. We turn the two axes into a calibrated policy that answers only when an answerability score and a correctness score each clear a separately certifies behave differently: the unanswerable-answer rate is controllable at every scale, while the wrong-answer rate is capped by model accuracy, so the guarantee tightens as threshold policy certifies both budgets at 0.75 coverage of correct answers, against 0.31 for a single threshold; at 14B it is the only policy that certifies at all.

</details>


### [36] [Cross-seed explainability using Procrustes-conditioned Joint End-to-end Top-K Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08499)
*Bendegúz Váradi,Zoltán Kmetty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于正交 Procrustes 旋转对齐的联合端到端 Top-K 稀疏自编码器，用于从独立训练的 BERT 模型中提取跨随机种子的通用特征。该方法先通过 Procrustes 旋转对齐不同种子的激活空间，再结合 Top-K 稀疏性、端到端下游任务优化以及辅助死特征复活损失进行联合训练。实验表明，该流程在三个基准数据集上均能产生更高通用性的特征（Pearson r ≥ 0.70），且高通用性特征编码可解释的社会语言学模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性中跨随机种子特征通用性是一个根本挑战：由于字典学习是非凸的，独立训练的网络会学习到错位的特征空间，导致表面上相同的特征可能因随机初始化而不同。现有的事后对齐基线方法效果有限，需要一种在训练前就对齐激活空间并联合学习通用特征的方法。

Method: 1) 计算不同随机种子 BERT 模型激活空间之间的正交 Procrustes 旋转矩阵，将激活投影到共享对齐空间；2) 在对齐后的激活上训练联合端到端 Top-K 稀疏自编码器，结合 Top-K 稀疏约束、端到端下游任务损失（如分类损失）以及基于先前 SAE 文献的辅助死特征复活损失；3) 在五组独立种子对（共十个 BERT 模型）上跨三个基准数据集（SST-2、Stanford Politeness、TweetEval Emotion）进行评估。

Result: 完整流程在所有三个数据集上均产生比事后对齐基线更通用的特征，跨种子 Pearson 相关系数 ≥ 0.70。定性分析确认高通用性特征编码可解释的社会语言学模式（如礼貌、情感、情感极性等）。

Conclusion: 通过 Procrustes 旋转预对齐激活空间并联合端到端训练 Top-K SAE，可有效解决跨随机种子特征错位问题，学习到高通用性且可解释的特征，为机制可解释性中的跨模型特征对齐提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present a Procrustes-conditioned Joint End-to-end Top-K Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) for extracting cross-seed universal features from independently trained BERT models. Cross-seed feature universality is a fundamental challenge in mechanistic interpretability: because dictionary learning is non-convex, independently trained networks learn misaligned feature spaces, so apparently identical features may differ by random initialization. We address this by computing an orthogonal Procrustes rotation between seeds' activation spaces before joint SAE training, combining Top-K sparsity, end-to-end downstream optimization, and an auxiliary dead-feature revival loss based on previous SAE literature. Evaluating on five independent seed pairs (ten BERT models) across three benchmark datasets (SST-2, Stanford Politeness, TweetEval Emotion), our full pipeline produces more universal features (Pearson r $\geq$ 0.70 across seeds) than post-hoc alignment baselines on all three datasets. A minimal qualitative analysis confirms that high-universality features encode interpretable sociolinguistic patterns.

</details>


### [37] [When the Judge Changes, So Does the Measurement: Auditing LLM-as-Judge Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08535)
*Zongyou Yang,Yinghan Hou,Xiaokun Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文将LLM-as-judge评估中的评估器替换歧视性视为度量效度问题，通过对比Qwen3密集评判模型（1.7B至32B参数）和MiniMax M2-M2.7 API的升级路径，发现评判模型升级不可互换：仅Qwen3 1.7B到4B带来稳健相邻增益，MiniMax相邻版本无显著提升；更强评判模型虽减弱但未消除位置和冗长偏差；重复采样陪审团在错误相关时增益有限；结构化辩论能大幅改变判决，但无解析器和回退日志无法归因于审议；建议报告包含数据集切片、偏差探针、错误依赖估计及协议审计轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM-as-judge评估中，评估器替换会导致分数变化，即使候选回答固定，这引入了评估器替换歧义，威胁测量效度。论文旨在系统研究评判模型升级路径的实际效果，量化偏差残留、陪审团机制及辩论协议的有效性，为构建可信赖的评估报告提供实证依据。

Method: 在四个判断数据集上，对比两条实用升级路径：1) Qwen3密集评判模型从1.7B扩展到32B参数；2) MiniMax M2到M2.7发布版API的跨版本迁移。实验包含相邻版本增益测试、位置/冗长偏差探针、重复采样陪审团误差相关性分析、结构化辩论协议对决策漂移的影响，并强调需配合解析器与回退日志进行归因。

Result: 主要模式：评判模型升级不可互换。仅Qwen3 1.7B→4B产生稳健相邻增益，MiniMax相邻版本无显著提升。更强评判模型减弱但未消除位置和冗长偏差。重复采样陪审团在错误相关时边际收益极小。结构化辩论能大幅移动判决，但缺乏解析器和回退日志无法将变化归因于审议过程。

Conclusion: LLM-as-judge评估报告必须包含数据集切片分析、偏差探针测试、错误依赖估计及协议审计轨迹，以确保测量效度与结果可解释性。单纯扩大模型规模或更换API版本不足以保证评估质量提升，需系统性协议设计与透明化报告。

Abstract: An LLM-as-judge score can move even when the candidate responses stay fixed, simply because the evaluator has changed. We treat this evaluator-replacement ambiguity as a measurement-validity problem. Across four judgment datasets, we compare two upgrade paths available in practice: scaling Qwen3 dense judges from 1.7B to 32B parameters and moving across MiniMax M2-M2.7 released APIs. The main pattern is that judge upgrades are not interchangeable: only Qwen3 1.7B to 4B gives a robust adjacent gain, while MiniMax adjacent releases do not. Stronger judges reduce but do not remove position and verbosity bias. Repeated-sample juries add little when errors are correlated. Structured debate can move decisions substantially, but without parser and fallback logs those shifts cannot be attributed to deliberation. We argue that LLM-as-judge reports should include dataset slices, bias probes, error-dependence estimates, and protocol audit trails.

</details>


### [38] [It Takes a MAESTRO To Prune Bad Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08601)
*Palaash Goel,Ayush Maheshwari,Tanmoy Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对稀疏激活的混合专家模型在部署时全量专家常驻内存的瓶颈，提出 MAESTRO 结构化剪枝框架，将自回归专家激活轨迹建模为遍历马尔可夫链，利用平稳分布捕捉跨层依赖，得到全局感知的重要性启发式，在 50% 压缩率下平均性能保持比基线提升 10.61%，且跨任务方差显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏激活 MoE 模型推理高效，但全量专家常驻内存造成部署瓶颈；现有结构化剪枝方法多针对密集 Transformer，基于局部启发式评估专家重要性，忽视了 MoE 路由的相互依赖性质。

Method: 提出 MAESTRO（基于马尔可夫链近似的专家稀疏化与转移路由），将自回归专家激活轨迹建模为遍历马尔可夫链，其平稳分布编码跨层依赖，从而得到全局感知的专家重要性启发式，指导结构化剪枝。

Result: 在安全、偏见、伦理等五大领域评测中，MAESTRO 在 50% 压缩率下平均性能保持比最先进基线提升高达 10.61%，且跨任务方差大幅降低，表明全局路由一致的剪枝能产生在异构任务上更一致泛化的模型。

Conclusion: 基于马尔可夫链建模的全局专家重要性评估能有效捕捉 MoE 跨层路由依赖，MAESTRO 实现了高压缩率下的高性能保持与低跨任务方差，为 MoE 模型高效部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Sparsely-activated Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models achieve remarkable inference efficiency by activating only a small fraction of parameters per token, yet their full expert banks reside in memory at all times, creating a prohibitive deployment bottleneck. Existing structured pruning methods, largely designed for dense transformers, assess expert importance using locally derived heuristics that are blind to the interdependent nature of MoE routing. We introduce MAESTRO (Markov-chain Approximated Expert Sparsification via Transition-based ROuting), a structured pruning framework designed for MoE architectures that models autoregressive expert activation trajectories as Ergodic Markov chains whose stationary distributions encode cross-layer dependencies, yielding a globally aware importance heuristic. Evaluated across five diverse domains including Safety, Bias, and Ethics, MAESTRO outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by up to 10.61% in average performance retention under a strict 50% compression regime, while exhibiting substantially lower cross-task variance, indicating that global, routing-congruent pruning produces models that generalize more consistently across heterogeneous tasks.

</details>


### [39] [DominoTree: Conditional Tree-Structured Drafting with Domino for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08642)
*Saw S. Lin,Jyh-Shing Roger Jang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DominoTree 是一种无需训练的最佳优先草稿树方法，结合 Domino 的条件非因子化修正，在 Qwen3 模型上实现高吞吐量和长接受长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码方法存在局限：块扩散草稿器（如 DFlash）仅建模每位置边缘分布；最佳优先树方法（如 DDTree）基于边缘扩展候选树；而 Domino 引入基于 GRU 的因果修正使草稿 token 分布具有路径依赖性，DDTree 的因子化公式无法表示这种结构。需要一种能利用 Domino 的路径依赖修正的树构建方法。

Method: DominoTree 采用最佳优先搜索构建草稿树，使用 Domino 的条件非因子化修正为每条根到节点路径评分，将每节点修正限制在候选 top-M 以保持实用。使用 GPU 原生 CUDA 图构建器实现逐位相同的树构建，保持接受率不变且构建开销低。

Result: 在 Qwen3-4B 上，DominoTree 达到高达 6.6x 加速，平均接受长度最高达 10.7 tokens/轮，在所有测试温度下优于其他方法。吞吐量比 Domino 解码器高 9-10%（Alpaca 上高达 +22%），在所有温度下优于 DDTree/CaDDTree。在 Qwen3-8B 上，保持最高接受长度，在 T=0 时比 DDTree 高 +24% 吞吐量，高温下与 DDTree/CaDDTree 持平或略低，但总体优于 DFlash 和 Domino。

Conclusion: DominoTree 通过结合 Domino 的路径依赖修正和高效 GPU 树构建，实现了最先进的推测解码性能，在多种模型规模和温度下均表现优异。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by drafting several tokens and verifying them in parallel. Block-diffusion drafters such as DFlash produce
  a draft block in one pass but model only per-position marginals; best-first tree methods such as DDTree expand candidate trees from those marginals. The
  released Domino drafter adds a GRU-based causal correction that makes each draft token's distribution path-dependent, a structure DDTree's factorized
  formulation cannot represent. We introduce DominoTree, a training-free best-first draft tree scored by Domino's conditional, non-factorized correction
  along each root-to-node path, made practical by restricting the per-node correction to a candidate top-M. On Qwen3-4B across eight benchmarks, DominoTree
  reaches up to 6.6x speedup over autoregressive decoding and the highest mean accept length of any evaluated method, up to 10.7 tokens per round, at every
  temperature we test. DominoTree constructs its tree with a GPU-native, CUDA-graph builder that is bit-identical to a reference Python implementation, so
  acceptance is unchanged, while keeping per-round tree construction cheap. With this builder as default, DominoTree wins throughput over the released
  Domino decoder at every temperature, 9-10% overall on Qwen3-4B and up to +22% on Alpaca, and over DDTree/CaDDTree at every temperature we test. On Qwen3-
  8B, DominoTree keeps the highest accepted length at every temperature and adds a decisive throughput win at T=0, +24% over DDTree; at higher temperature
  that edge over DDTree/CaDDTree narrows to a tie and a small loss, while its Overall aggregate wins over DFlash and Domino persist.

</details>


### [40] [UltraX: Refining Pre-Training Data at Scale with Adaptive Programmatic Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08646)
*Xinlong Zhao,Dongsheng Liu,Hengyu Zhao,Zixuan Fu,Zheng Wang,Jie Cai,Jie Zhou,Qiang Ma,Xuanhe Zhou,Xu Han,Yudong Wang,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UltraX 是一个面向大规模预训练数据的函数调用精炼框架，通过引入插入、删除、修改三种编辑操作，构建可靠的程序监督生成管道，实现细粒度实例级编辑，显著提升数据精炼的质量、效率和可靠性，在更少训练 token 下达到或超越基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着训练数据逼近物理极限，Scaling Law 红利递减，提升大语言模型性能更依赖高质量数据利用。现有精炼方法在大规模语料下存在质量、效率、可靠性瓶颈：基于规则的方法受固定启发式约束，难以处理实例级变化；基于 LLM 的方法虽提升质量，但难以满足大规模数据处理的效率与可靠性需求。

Method: UltraX 提出函数调用精炼框架，补全编辑函数空间（引入插入操作），实现细粒度实例级编辑。核心组件包括：1) 数据集自适应提示优化，引导专家 LLM 生成高质量端到端精炼文本；2) 行对齐映射与动态上下文替换，将原文-精炼文本对转化为结构化程序监督；3) 低置信度样本过滤与操作组合比例控制采样，提升监督质量并稳定训练分布；4) 推理执行阶段采用滑动窗口预测、全局操作聚合与系统化后处理，规范化验证模型输出，增强大规模执行的稳定性与可靠性。

Result: 实验表明 UltraX 在所有语料上平均性能最高，且在更少训练 token 下匹配或超越基线，展现出更强的数据效率和精炼可靠性。

Conclusion: UltraX 通过完善编辑操作空间、构建可靠的程序监督生成管道以及稳健的推理执行机制，有效解决了大规模预训练数据精炼中质量、效率、可靠性难以兼顾的问题，为数据高效利用提供了新范式。

Abstract: As available training data approaches its physical limit, gains from Scaling Laws have begun to diminish. Consequently, improving Large Language Models (LLMs) now depends less on data expansion and more on higher-quality data utilization. However, in the context of large-scale corpora, existing refinement methodologies face significant limitations in quality, efficiency, and reliability: Rule-based approaches are constrained by fixed heuristics and struggle with instance-level variations; LLM-based approaches improve quality but fail to meet the efficiency and reliability requirements of large-scale data processing. To address these challenges, we propose UltraX, a function-calling refinement framework for large-scale pre-training data that completes the editing function space by introducing insertion in addition to deletion and modification, enabling fine-grained instance-level editing. Specifically, UltraX builds a reliable program-supervision generation pipeline. In this pipeline, dataset-adaptive prompt optimization first guides an expert LLM to produce high-quality end-to-end refined texts, and Line Alignment Mapping and Dynamic Context Replacement then convert original-refined text pairs into structured program supervision. Meanwhile, UltraX improves supervision quality and stabilizes the training distribution with low-confidence example filtering and ratio-controlled sampling by operation combination. During inference and execution, it normalizes and validates model outputs through sliding-window prediction, global operation aggregation, and systematic post-processing, improving the stability and reliability of large-scale execution. Experiments show that UltraX achieves the highest average performance across all corpora and also matches or surpasses baselines with fewer training tokens, demonstrating stronger data efficiency and refinement reliability.

</details>


### [41] [WebSwarm: Recursive Multi-Agent Orchestration for Deep-and-Wide Web Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08662)
*Xiaoshuai Song,Liancheng Zhang,Kangzhi Zhao,Yutao Zhu,Zhongyuan Wang,Guanting Dong,Jinghan Yang,Han Li,Kun Gai,Ji-Rong Wen,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WebSwarm是一个渐进式递归委托框架，用于大语言模型（LLM）驱动的网络搜索智能体。它通过动态实例化带有局部目标和搜索模式的智能体搜索节点，支持节点间的递归分解、协作与证据回传，解决了单智能体和现有多智能体系统在深度、广度、协作自适应性和证据驱动扩展方面的不足。实验表明WebSwarm在深度、广度及深广交织任务上持续超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的网络搜智能体多为单一ReAct风格，受限于单一长轨迹和有限上下文，难以同时兼顾搜索深度和覆盖面。现有多智能体系统虽通过并行聚合提升覆盖，但在递归深度、协作自适应性和基于证据的扩展上仍有明显短板。因此需要一种能在推理过程中联合构建任务分解、递归扩展和智能体协作的框架。

Method: WebSwarm采用渐进式递归委托机制：动态实例化智能体搜索节点，每个节点耦合一个局部目标和一种搜索模式（规定如何组织搜索与协作）。节点可自行求解目标，也可进一步委托子节点；求解后将证据和结果向上回传，父节点据此进一步扩展、修正或聚合。为引导该过程，WebSwarm首先探测任务相关信息在网络上的组织方式以奠定后续节点扩展基础，并跨同质兄弟节点复用过程级经验。

Result: 在BrowseComp-Plus、WideSearch、DeepWideSearch和GISA四个基准上，WebSwarm在深度、广度及深广交织任务上均持续优于单智能体和多智能体基线。消融实验、任务难度分析、网络工具效率及模型泛化性实验进一步解释了其有效性，并为多智能体搜索系统提供了洞见。

Conclusion: WebSwarm通过渐进式递归委托、动态节点实例化、证据回传机制以及基于网络信息组织的探测与经验复用，有效解决了LLM网络搜索智能体在深度、广度、协作自适应性和证据驱动扩展方面的局限，显著提升了复杂搜索任务的性能，为未来多智能体搜索系统设计提供了可借鉴的范式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based web search agents are transforming information seeking from simple factoid question answering into complex, deep-and-wide search and research-oriented tasks. A single ReAct-style agent is constrained by one long trajectory and limited context, making it difficult to handle depth and coverage simultaneously. Existing multi-agent systems improve search coverage through parallel execution and aggregation, but still exhibit clear limitations in recursive depth, collaboration adaptability, and evidence-grounded expansion. We propose WebSwarm, a progressive recursive delegation framework that jointly constructs task decomposition, recursive expansion, and agent collaboration during inference. WebSwarm dynamically instantiates agentic search nodes, each coupling a local objective with a search mode that specifies how the node should organize search and collaboration. Each node can either solve its objective itself or further delegate child nodes; after solving, it returns evidence and results upward, enabling parent nodes to further expand, revise, or aggregate the search process. To guide this process, WebSwarm first probes how task-relevant information is organized on the web to ground subsequent node expansion, and reuses process-level experience across homogeneous sibling nodes. Experiments on BrowseComp-Plus, WideSearch, DeepWideSearch, and GISA show that WebSwarm consistently outperforms single-agent and multi-agent baselines on deep, wide, and interleaved deep-and-wide tasks. Further analyses of ablation, task difficulty, web tool efficiency, and model generalization explain WebSwarm's effectiveness and provide insights for multi-agent search systems.

</details>


### [42] [Do You Need a Frontier Model as a Citation Verifier? Benchmarking Rubric LLMs for Deep-Research Source Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08700)
*Ethan Leung,Elias Lumer,Corey Feld,Austin Huber,Vamse Kumar Subbiah,Kevin Paul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在深度研究系统中使用大语言模型（LLM）作为评判员来评估引用质量的校准问题，发现较便宜的模型（如GPT-5-mini）在源相关性和事实支撑两个维度上均能达到具有竞争力的性能，但不同模型在通过率漂移、假阳性率和假阴性率上存在显著差异，标量F1掩盖了这种方向性偏差，而这正是下游强化学习循环会强化的内容。因此，在将引用评分标准用作奖励信号前，必须对评判员进行校准，且这种校准不需要最昂贵的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着强化学习越来越依赖LLM评判员为每个评分标准打分并作为训练时的奖励模型，在信任这种信号前，需要了解评判员必须具备何种能力及其偏差程度。本文针对深度研究系统中的引用质量这一结构化评分任务（包含源相关性和事实支撑两个需要LLM判断的维度）开展校准研究。

Method: 在对抗性长文本基准上，对来自3个模型家族的8个现成LLM评判员进行评测，共计1,248个评分决策，全部经人工复核，其中378个困难案例由评判员分歧裁决产生。评估指标包括通过类F1、Cohen's kappa、通过率漂移、假阳性率、假阴性率等。

Result: GPT-5-mini在源相关性上达到最强通过类F1（0.908，κ=0.636）；在事实支撑上，各评判员统计上无显著差异（置信区间重叠），无单一模型占优。在可比F1下，评判员在通过率漂移、假阳性率、假阴性率上仍有显著差异。标量F1掩盖了方向性偏差，而这正是下游RL循环会强化的内容。

Conclusion: 校准评判员是将引用评分标准用作奖励信号的前提条件，且实验结果表明这种校准不需要最昂贵的可用模型。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning increasingly relies on an LLM judge to score each rubric criterion, and that judge acts as the reward model during training. Before such a signal can be trusted, we need to know how capable the judge must be and how biased it is. We study this calibration question for citation quality in deep-research systems, where a search-grounded LLM must support each claim it writes with a cited source. Citation quality is a structured rubric task in which each attribution-citation pair is judged along two dimensions that require an LLM, source relevance and factual support. On an adversarial long-form benchmark, we score 8 off-the-shelf LLM judges from 3 model families against gold labels over 1,248 rubric decisions, all of which were human-reviewed and 378 of which were hard cases adjudicated from judge disagreements. Cheaper judges remain competitive across both dimensions, with GPT-5-mini attaining the strongest source-relevance pass-class F1 at 0.908 ($κ$=0.636), while on factual support the judges are statistically indistinguishable (overlapping confidence intervals), so no single model dominates. At comparable F1, the judges still differ substantially in pass-rate drift, false positive rate, and false negative rate. Scalar F1 obscures this directional bias, yet it is exactly what a downstream reinforcement learning loop would reinforce. Calibrating the judge is therefore a prerequisite for using citation rubrics as reward signals, and our results show that this calibration does not require the most expensive available model.

</details>


### [43] [Validity of LLMs as data annotators: AMALIA on authority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08731)
*Manuel Pita*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 葡萄牙国家语言模型 AMALIA-9B 在权威道德基础编码任务上虽与人类编码员一致性较高，但通过分解提示词的“恢复差距”测试发现，其仅能恢复约一半的整体性能，且依赖表层相关特征（如权威人物附近的道德愤慨），而非遵循理论构建。同语料下多语言开源模型能弥合该差距，说明问题出在模型本身。研究主张主权大模型基准测试应同时考察与人类一致性及证据路径的合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国家语言模型被视为语言共同体测量公民言论与价值观的工具，但现有评估多仅关注与人类编码员的一致性（可靠性），忽视了模型是否真正遵循理论构建（效度）。本文旨在通过“恢复差距”方法，检验 AMALIA-9B 在权威道德基础这一需推断的理论构建上，是否依据理论规则而非表层捷径做出判断，并探讨英语校准工具能否迁移至葡萄牙语模型。

Method: 采用恢复差距实验设计：将整体提示词分解为代码本的原子子句，再按理论显式规则重组，对比分解后与整体提示词的性能差距。若校准能缩小差距，说明模型遵循理论且具可迁移性；否则提示构建-模型工具失效。实验在同一葡萄牙语语料上，分别测试 AMALIA-9B 与开源多语言大模型，并进行错误分析。

Result: AMALIA-9B 整体 F1 与人类编码员相差仅 6 分，但分解提示词后性能下降约一半，恢复差距显著。错误分析显示模型依赖“权威人物附近的道德愤慨”等表层相关特征。同语料下开源多语言大模型能弥合该差距，排除了语料本身的主要解释力。AMALIA 仍可用于大规模筛选与预编码，但无法独立胜任该构建的精确测量。

Conclusion: 单一反例不足以否定国家模型整体价值，但揭示了当前主权大模型基准测试的盲区：仅考察与人类一致性不足，必须同时验证模型达成一致的证据路径是否符合理论构建。未来基准应纳入恢复差距等效度导向指标，确保模型不仅“答对”，而且“因对而对”。

Abstract: A national language model offers a linguistic community its own instrument for measuring what its citizens say and value. Portugal's AMALIA, a publicly funded 9B-parameter model for European Portuguese, appears competitive on agreement alone: asked to code the moral foundation of authority, it agrees with trained human coders to within six F1 points of open models eight to thirteen times its size. Yet agreement is reliability, not validity. For theoretical constructs that must be inferred rather than read from surface features, the question is whether the model follows the construct's theory or reaches the right code by correlated shortcuts. We test this with the recovery gap: the loss in performance when a holistic prompt is decomposed into the codebook's atomic clauses and recombined by the theory's explicit rule. If calibration closes that gap, some portability should survive across models and languages; where it does not, the construct-model instrument is the likely locus of failure. We ask whether a calibrated English instrument transfers to AMALIA-9B and to European Portuguese. For one construct and one corpus, it does not. Decomposition recovers only about half of AMALIA's holistic performance, and error analysis suggests reliance on surface correlates, especially moral outrage near authority figures. An open multilingual LLM closes the gap on the same Portuguese corpus under the same instructions, pointing away from the corpus as the main explanation. AMALIA can still screen and pre-code at scale, but it cannot yet measure this construct well enough to stand alone. The study is a single counterexample, not a verdict on national models; it argues that sovereign-LLM benchmark batteries should test not only agreement with human coders, but the evidential route by which that agreement is warranted.

</details>


### [44] [UniClawBench: A Universal Benchmark for Proactive Agents on Real-World Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08768)
*Zhekai Chen,Chengqi Duan,Kaiyue Sun,Bohao Li,Yuqing Wang,Manyuan Zhang,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniClawBench 是首个面向动态真实环境的能力驱动基准，基于五大核心能力设计 400 个双语任务，采用实时 Docker 容器与闭环评估策略，分离模型能力与框架设计，揭示二者对智能体性能的联合影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准依赖沙箱环境、单轮评估、场景化任务分类混淆多种能力，难以定位智能体失败根因。

Method: 构建 UniClawBench，基于五大基础能力设计 400 双语任务；在实时 Docker 容器中采用细粒度逐步完成检查点评估；设计闭环评估策略（执行者、隐藏监督者、用户代理）模拟多轮人类反馈；在多个智能体框架下评估 SOTA 模型，分离基础模型能力与框架设计选择。

Result: 通过跨模型和框架的全面对比，展示了基础模型能力与智能体框架设计如何共同塑造真实环境下的性能。

Conclusion: UniClawBench 为主动智能体在动态真实环境中的评估提供了新范式，代码与基准已开源。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models and multimodal large language models has accelerated the emergence of proactive agents capable of operating everyday tools and assisting users in real-world environments. However, existing benchmarks struggle to evaluate such agents effectively, as they often rely on sandboxed environments and single-turn evaluation paradigms. Moreover, their scenario-based task taxonomies mix multiple model capabilities within the same task category, making it difficult to identify the root causes of agent failures. To address these limitations, we introduce UniClawBench, the first capability-driven benchmark designed to evaluate proactive agents in dynamic, real-world settings. UniClawBench is built around five foundational model capabilities: Skill Usage, Exploration, Long-Context Reasoning, Multimodal Understanding, and Cross-Platform Coordination. Based on these capabilities, we design 400 bilingual real-world tasks. Unlike previous benchmarks that rely on static, pre-recorded answers, our benchmark evaluates agents in live Docker containers using fine-grained, step-by-step completion checkpoints. Furthermore, we design a closed-loop evaluation strategy comprising an executor agent, a hidden supervisor agent, and a user agent to simulate realistic multi-turn human feedback without leaking grading criteria. To disentangle base model capabilities from framework-level design choices, we evaluate state-of-the-art models under multiple agent frameworks. Through comprehensive comparisons across both models and frameworks, we show how base model capabilities and agent framework designs jointly shape performance in real-world environments. To facilitate future research, we make our benchmark and code publicly available at https://github.com/HKU-MMLab/UniClawBench.

</details>

### [45] [Towards the Explainability of Temporal Graph Networks via Memory Backtracking and Topological Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07716)
*Yazheng Liu,Xi Zhang,Sihong Xie,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种针对时序图网络（TGN）的解释方法，通过拓扑归因树和记忆回溯树量化历史事件对预测的影响，并应用层级相关性传播（LRP）保证归因总和等于模型logits，设计优化目标解决top-k选择因非线性映射导致的不忠实问题，在九个时序图数据集上实验表明该方法提供忠实解释且优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序图网络的解释方法忽略了记忆模块——TGN的核心组件，该模块记录并更新节点历史，导致过去事件的影响未被探索。理解哪些历史事件驱动模型预测可增强TGN的可信度。

Method: 提出拓扑归因树捕捉邻居及其记忆向量的影响，记忆回溯树量化历史事件如何塑造节点记忆向量。在TGN中应用层级相关性传播（LRP），确保事件总贡献等于模型logits。针对top-k选择因logits到概率的非线性映射可能导致不忠实问题，设计优化目标识别重要事件。

Result: 在九个时序图数据集上，涵盖节点属性预测、链接预测和图分类任务，实验表明该方法提供忠实解释，且优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 该工作首次通过拓扑归因树和记忆回溯树实现了对TGN预测的完整归因，结合LRP保证归因守恒，并通过优化目标解决非线性映射带来的不忠实问题，为时序图网络的可解释性提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Temporal graphs are ubiquitous in real-world applications and Temporal Graph Networks (TGNs) have achieved superior predictive accuracy. Understanding which historical events drive model predictions can enhance trustworthiness of TGNs. Existing explanation methods overlook the memory module, the core component that records and updates node histories, leaving the influence of past events unexplored. To address this, we attribute TGNs predictions through the topology attribution tree and memory backtracking tree. The topology attribution tree captures the influence of neighbors and their memory vectors, then the memory backtracking tree quantifies how historical events shape node memory vectors. We apply the LRP in TGNs, ensuring that the total contribution of events equals the logits of model. Finally, top-k selection may be unfaithful due to the nonlinear mapping from logits to probabilities, we design optimization objectives to identify the important events. Experiments on nine temporal graph datasets, spanning node property prediction, link prediction tasks and graph classification tasks, show that our method provides faithful explanations and outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. The code is available at https://github.com/yazhengliu/MemExplainer

</details>


### [46] [Who Gets Missed in the Tail? Thresholded Subgroup Underdiagnosis in Long-Tailed Chest X-ray Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07717)
*Ha-Hieu Pham,Hai-Dang Nguyen,Dang P. M. Cao,Thanh-Huy Nguyen,Min Xu,Trung-Nghia Le,Ulas Bagci,Huy-Hieu Pham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究长尾多标签胸部X射线分类模型在分数转决策后的部署前公平性问题，发现罕见标签的公平性取决于发现、亚群和操作阈值的联合作用，而非仅靠标签频率或排序指标。通过诊断梯度方法，在VinDr-CXR和MIMIC-CXR数据集上，组尾加权结合尾感知阈值选择显著降低了尾部假阴性率和最差亚群假阴性率，同时提升了宏平均mAP。配对bootstrap对比和GroupDRO参考实验支持了阈值调整的有效性，但残留漏报率仍较高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决胸部X射线分类中长尾分布导致的罕见阳性患者在亚群中被遗漏的部署前公平性问题，即模型排序性能可接受但阈值决策后罕见发现和弱势亚群漏报率高。

Method: 采用诊断梯度方法分离类别级长尾损失、亚群感知加权、群体鲁棒性和阈值选择。在VinDr-CXR和MIMIC-CXR/CXR-LT数据集上，应用组尾加权和尾感知阈值调整，使用配对bootstrap对比验证阈值化FNR降低，并运行GroupDRO作为群体鲁棒性基线。

Result: VinDr-CXR上尾部FNR从0.665降至0.269，性别最差群体FNR从0.705降至0.157，年龄最差群体FNR从0.822降至0.133，macro-mAP从0.611升至0.635。MIMIC-CXR上尾部FNR从0.866降至0.741，性别、年龄、种族、保险最差群体FNR均下降，但残留漏报率仍高。配对bootstrap支持显著性，GroupDRO表明仅靠群体鲁棒性不足以消除罕见亚群漏报。

Conclusion: 罕见标签公平性在胸部X射线分类中联合依赖于具体发现、亚群身份和操作阈值，不能仅通过标签频率或排序指标保证；阈值感知干预能显著降低漏报但残留风险仍需关注。

Abstract: In chest X-ray (CXR) classification, acceptable ranking performance can still leave rare-positive patients below threshold, especially within subgroups. We study this pre-deployment fairness problem as an audit question: after a long-tailed multi-label CXR model is converted from scores into decisions, who is missed? Across VinDr-CXR and MIMIC-CXR/CXR-LT, we use a diagnostic ladder to separate class-level long-tail losses, subgroup-aware weighting, group robustness, and threshold selection. On VinDr-CXR, group-tail weighting followed by tail-aware thresholding reduces tail FNR from 0.665 to 0.269, sex worst-group FNR from 0.705 to 0.157, and age worst-group FNR from 0.822 to 0.133, while macro-mAP increases from 0.611 to 0.635. On MIMIC-CXR/CXR-LT, the same score-to-threshold comparison reduces tail FNR from 0.866 to 0.741 and lowers worst-group FNR across sex, age, race, and insurance; residual missed-positive rates nevertheless remain high. Paired bootstrap contrasts on VinDr support the thresholded FNR reductions, and GroupDRO reference runs indicate that aggregate group robustness alone does not remove rare subgroup misses in this setting. The study supports a narrow audit claim: rare-label fairness in CXR depends jointly on the finding, subgroup, and operating threshold, not on label frequency or ranking metrics alone.

</details>


### [47] [LLT: Local Linear Transformer for PDE Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07718)
*Oded Ovadia,Eli Turkel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出局部线性Transformer（LLT）用于PDE算子学习，结合线性全局注意力与局部空间混合，并引入坐标和几何信息，在多种PDE问题（弹性、塑性、翼型流动、管道流动、达西流动）及不同离散化（有限元、有限体积、有限差分）和网格类型（结构化、非结构化）上实现了具有竞争力或更低的相对L2误差，训练迭代耗时比Transolver降低1.8-2.5倍，并在三维汽车空气动力学数据集（32,186个非结构化网格点）上验证了可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子已成为学习PDE解映射和加速数值模拟的常用方法。基于Transformer的神经算子因注意力机制能学习计算域中的长程依赖而备受关注，但标准注意力存在两大局限：随计算节点数二次方扩展，且缺乏对局部交互的显式偏置。

Method: 提出局部线性Transformer（LLT）架构，融合线性全局注意力与局部空间混合，并显式注入坐标和几何信息，以同时捕捉长程依赖和局部交互，降低计算复杂度。

Result: 在弹性、塑性、翼型流动、管道流动、达西流动等多个PDE基准上，LLT相对L2误差优于或持平现有神经算子和Transformer基线；在结构化网格上每训练迭代耗时比Transolver降低1.8-2.5倍；成功扩展至含32,186个非结构化网格点的三维汽车空气动力学数据集。

Conclusion: LLT在不同离散化方法、网格类型和问题设置下均表现出高精度和计算高效的PDE算子学习能力，为大规模科学计算提供了实用的Transformer变体。

Abstract: Neural operators have become a common approach for learning PDE solution maps and accelerating numerical simulations. Transformer-based neural operators are of particular interest, since attention can learn long-range dependencies in the computational domain. However, standard attention has two major limitations when applied to PDEs: it scales quadratically with the number of computational nodes, and it lacks an explicit bias toward local interactions. To address these issues, we introduce Local Linear Transformer (LLT) for PDE operator learning. The architecture combines linear global attention with local spatial mixing, and incorporates coordinate and geometry information. We evaluate LLT on several PDE problems, including elasticity, plasticity, airfoil flow, pipe flow, and Darcy flow. The reference data for these problems span finite-element, finite-volume, and finite-difference discretizations on structured and unstructured meshes. Compared with other neural-operator and transformer baselines from prior studies, LLT achieves competitive or lower relative $L_2$ error across these problems. On matched structured discretizations, wall-clock time per training iteration is reduced by factors of 1.8 to 2.5 relative to Transolver. We also scale the approach and apply it to a three-dimensional car aerodynamics dataset with 32,186 unstructured mesh points per sample. Together, these results indicate that LLT provides an accurate and computationally efficient operator for PDE problems across discretizations, mesh types, and problem settings.

</details>


### [48] [ReCoLoRA: Spectrum-Aware Recursive Consolidation for Continual LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07719)
*Wentao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReCoLoRA提出了一种基于谱感知的递归合并低秩适配器框架，通过在每个新任务前重新分解当前有效权重（而非原始预训练权重），将模型分解为冻结残差、缓慢更新的主成分和全新适配器，从而实现持续微调中有效缓解灾难性遗忘，在六任务GLUE序列上以更少可训练参数超越LoRA、PiSSA、AdaLoRA、DoRA等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调（如LoRA）在任务序列上持续堆叠低秩更新至同一冻结权重，导致新任务倾向于覆盖旧任务知识，造成灾难性遗忘，缺乏一种能在持续学习中保持前序任务性能且参数高效的方法。

Method: ReCoLoRA采用谱感知初始化：从预训练权重的随机SVD初始化适配器，用肘部准则选取各层有效秩，先适配主子空间再开放残差容量；在每个新任务前，对当前有效权重（而非原始权重）进行递归重分解，得到冻结残差、缓慢更新的主成分和全新适配器，使每个任务均从已吸收前序任务知识的模型起步。

Result: 在四个7-8B骨干网络上的六任务持续GLUE基准中，ReCoLoRA在三个骨干上获得最佳最终平均分，且训练参数更少；提供的预言路由任务库变体作为完全任务隔离的上界。

Conclusion: ReCoLoRA通过递归合并低秩适配器，有效解决了持续参数高效微调中的灾难性遗忘问题，在保持参数效率的同时实现了优于现有方法的持续学习性能。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning adapts a large language model to one task cheaply, but across a task sequence LoRA-style methods keep stacking low-rank updates on the same frozen weight, so each new task tends to overwrite the previous ones. We present ReCoLoRA (Recursive Consolidation of Low-Rank Adapters), a spectrum-aware framework for continual fine-tuning: adapters are initialized from a randomized SVD of the pretrained weight, per-layer effective ranks are selected by an elbow criterion, and the principal subspace is adapted before residual capacity is opened. Before each new task, ReCoLoRA re-decomposes the current effective weight, rather than the original one, into a frozen residual, a slowly updated principal component, and a fresh adapter (recursive consolidation), so every task starts from the model that has already absorbed its predecessors. On a six-task continual GLUE sequence over four 7-8B backbones, ReCoLoRA attains the best final average score on three of the four backbones against rank-swept LoRA, PiSSA, AdaLoRA, and DoRA baselines while training fewer parameters; an oracle-routed task-bank variant serves as an upper bound under full task isolation. Code: https://github.com/bhqy666/ReCoLoRA.

</details>


### [49] [Omni-Sleep: A Sleep Foundation Model via Hierarchical Contrastive Learning of CNS--ANS Dynamic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07720)
*Zhoujie Hou,Song Wang,Kexin Lou,Mo Wang,Chen Wei,Quanying Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Omni-Sleep是一种睡眠基础模型，利用中枢/自主神经系统划分作为生理学先验进行拓扑约束表示学习，在100,000+小时多中心多模态PSG数据上预训练，在睡眠分期和多疾病分类任务上优于基线，展示了生理层次结构对泛化睡眠表示学习的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有睡眠基础模型以拓扑无关方式融合异构生物信号（EEG、EOG、EMG、ECG、呼吸等），忽视了其生理组织结构；睡眠生理学源于中枢神经系统(CNS)和自主神经系统(ANS)的协同动态，亟需利用生理层次先验进行结构化表示学习。

Method: 提出Omni-Sleep模型，采用CNS/ANS分区作为生理学先验，通过三个目标学习结构化表示：(1)系统内一致性，捕捉神经和心肺信号内的共享子系统因素；(2)系统间同步，对齐子系统轨迹建模脑-体动态；(3)潜在空间掩码时序建模，捕捉长程睡眠动态。在100,000+小时多中心多模态PSG数据上预训练。

Result: 在睡眠分期和多疾病分类任务上，跨数据集和模态消融设置下，Omni-Sleep优于强基线模型，展现出更高标签效率、跨数据集泛化能力和对缺失模态的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了生理层次结构对泛化睡眠表示学习的价值，Omni-Sleep为睡眠基础模型提供了生理学指导的拓扑约束学习范式。

Abstract: Sleep physiology arises from the coordinated dynamics of the central nervous system (CNS) and autonomic nervous system (ANS), as reflected by multimodal polysomnography signals including EEG, EOG, EMG, ECG, and respiration. However, existing sleep foundation models often fuse heterogeneous biosignals in a topology-agnostic manner, overlooking their physiological organization. We introduce Omni-Sleep, a sleep foundation model that uses the CNS/ANS partition as a physiological prior for topology-constrained representation learning. Omni-Sleep learns structured representations through three objectives: intra-system consistency, which captures shared subsystem-level factors within neural and cardio-respiratory signals; inter-system synchronization, which aligns subsystem trajectories to model brain--body dynamics; and latent-space masked temporal modeling, which captures long-horizon sleep dynamics. Pre-trained on over 100,000 hours of multi-center multimodal PSG data, Omni-Sleep is evaluated on sleep staging and multi-disease classification. Across datasets and modality-ablation settings, Omni-Sleep outperforms strong foundation-model baselines, showing improved label efficiency, cross-dataset generalization, and robustness to missing modalities. These results highlight the value of physiological hierarchy for generalizable sleep representation learning. Code is available at https://github.com/AutoBrain-sleep/OmniSleep.

</details>


### [50] [Uncertainty-gated selection for block-sparse attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07724)
*Thomas Rossi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于信息价值的路由器，用于改进块稀疏注意力中的 top-k 选择机制。该路由器通过测量每个查询的 top-k 切割决策的果断程度，在得分差距最小的查询上加倍保留的键块集合。该方法与现有骨干网络和块评分方法（如 Quest）兼容，在多个长上下文基准上显著提升召回率，同时保持较高的推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的块稀疏注意力通过 top-k 选择键块来替代 O(N^2) 的 softmax，但这种硬切割是短视的：当第 k 个和第 k+1 个块的得分非常接近时，选择器会在不增加预算的情况下强行决定，导致包含答案证据的块被丢弃且无法在后续层恢复。

Method: 提出一个价值信息路由器，为每个查询计算 top-k 切割的决策间隙（gap），即第 k 个与第 k+1 个块得分的差异。对于间隙最小（决策最不果断）的查询，将保留的键块集合加倍。该路由器与骨干网络无关，可叠加在现有的块评分方法（如 Quest）之上，并融合了选择与内核的流水线以加速推理。

Result: 在 LongBench-v2 medium (n=215) 上，router-on-Quest 的配对召回率达到 0.75，比 top-k 基线的 0.47 提升 28 个百分点 (McNemar p<0.01)，并在 RULER NIAH multikey 任务上与密集注意力相差仅 2 个百分点。在四个模型（Qwen2.5、Mistral-Nemo、Qwen3.6 等）上复现。在 128K 上下文长度下，Qwen2.5-7B-1M 和 Qwen3.6 分别保留了密集注意力准确率的 0.81 和 0.89（而 SSA-style top-k 仅 0.09）。融合流水线的推理耗时为密集注意力的 0.62x 和 0.80x。

Conclusion: 价值信息路由器通过自适应扩展保留键块集合，有效缓解了块稀疏注意力中硬 top-k 选择导致的证据丢失问题，在不牺牲太多计算效率的前提下，显著提升了长上下文语言模型的检索和推理性能，且具有良好的通用性和可复现性。

Abstract: Block-sparse attention scales long-context language models by replacing the O(N^2) softmax with a per-query top-k selection over key blocks. This cutoff is myopic: when the k-th and (k+1)-th blocks are nearly tied in score, the selector commits without spending extra budget, and a dropped block carrying answer evidence is unrecoverable downstream. We propose a value-of-information router that measures, for each query, how decisively the top-k cut was made, and doubles the kept set for the queries where that gap is smallest; the rule is backbone-agnostic and stacks with existing block-scoring methods such as Quest. On LongBench-v2 medium at n=215 (the entire dataset subset), router-on-Quest reaches paired recall 0.75 vs. top-k 0.47 -- +28 pp over the SSA-style baseline (McNemar p<0.01) -- and lands within 2 pp of dense on RULER NIAH multikey at the same context. The lift reproduces on four models from three architectures (Qwen2.5, Mistral-Nemo, Qwen3.6). At 128K, the router preserves 0.81 and 0.89 of dense accuracy on Qwen2.5-7B-1M and Qwen3.6 (vs. SSA-style top-k at 0.09 on the former) while the fused selection-plus-kernel pipeline runs at 0.62x and 0.80x dense wall time.

</details>


### [51] [SHIFT: Survival Prediction from Incomplete and Heterogeneous Genomic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07725)
*Muhammet Sami Yavuz,Ayhan Can Erdur,Sabri Mustafa Kahya,Benedikt Wiestler,Jana Lipkova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SHIFT是一种基于Transformer的缺失感知生存预测模型，能直接处理不完整的基因组输入而无需测试时插补，通过掩码自注意力和特征可用性掩码仅基于观测输入进行预测，并在训练中引入变速率特征掩码以增强对异质缺失模式的鲁棒性。在胶质母细胞瘤和肺鳞状细胞癌的多中心外部验证中，SHIFT展现出强泛化能力，优于标准生存基线和插补方法，且单一模型即可适应不同特征集；研究还发现纳入不完整队列患者可提升外部数据表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基因组预测模型难以跨机构迁移，主因是不同机构测序面板差异导致结构性特征缺失。现有应对策略（限制共享基因、排除不完整患者、测试时插补）均会降低鲁棒性并限制多中心数据利用。

Method: 提出SHIFT模型：1) 将每个基因组特征单独表示；2) 采用掩码自注意力机制配合特征可用性掩码，使预测仅基于观测输入；3) 训练阶段引入变速率特征掩码，增强对异质缺失模式的鲁棒性。无需测试时插补，单一模型即可处理不同特征集。

Result: 在胶质母细胞瘤和肺鳞癌多队列外部验证（含严重跨队列面板错配场景）中，SHIFT表现出强泛化能力，优于标准生存基线和插补方法。且发现在开发阶段纳入不完整队列患者可提升外部数据性能，表明部分观测队列无需排除。

Conclusion: 缺失感知建模是精准肿瘤学中多中心生存预测的实用策略，SHIFT通过直接处理不完整基因组输入实现了跨机构的鲁棒预测。

Abstract: Genomic prediction models often fail to transfer across institutions because sequencing panels differ across sites, creating structural feature missingness at deployment. Existing approaches to this challenge typically restrict analysis to genes shared across cohorts, exclude patients with incomplete profiles, or rely on test-time imputation, all of which can reduce robustness and limit the use of multi-center data. We propose Survival prediction Handling Incomplete Features using Transformer (SHIFT), a missingness-aware survival model that directly predicts from incomplete genomic inputs without test-time imputation. SHIFT represents each genomic feature separately and uses masked self-attention, along with a feature-availability mask, so that predictions are based only on observed inputs. Further, we introduce variable-rate feature masking during training to improve robustness to heterogeneous missingness patterns. We evaluate the approach on glioblastoma and lung squamous cell carcinoma with external validation across multiple cohorts, including a challenging setting with severe cross-cohort panel mismatch. Across these settings, SHIFT shows strong generalization and compares favorably with standard survival baselines and imputation-based approaches, while using a single model across differing feature sets. We also find that incorporating patients from incomplete cohorts during development can improve performance on external data, suggesting that partially observed cohorts need not be excluded from model building. These results support missingness-aware modeling as a practical strategy for multi-center survival prediction in precision oncology.

</details>


### [52] [Jet-Long: Efficient Long-Context Extension with Dynamic Bifocal RoPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07740)
*Haozhan Tang,Zerui Wang,Yuxian Gu,Song Han,Han Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Jet-Long 是一种无需调优的零样本长上下文扩展方法，采用双焦点机制：局部 RoPE 忠实窗口与动态自适应缩放因子的长程窗口相结合，在短输入上完全恢复基座模型，在长输入上干净外推；配合包含-排除注意力融合与即时 RoPE 修正旋转，融合为单个 CuTe 内核后，预填充吞吐达 FA2 的 1.39 倍，单批生成开销 ≤4%。在 Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B 上下文 128K 实验中，RULER 分数分别领先最强基线 +4.79/+2.18/+2.03 pp，HELMET-RAG 总体准确率最优，PG-19 困惑度最低；还能泛化到混合注意力架构（如 Jet-Nemotron）且超参数稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 LLM 在长上下文应用（检索增强生成、仓库级编码、智能体工作流）中部署日益增多，积累的推理与工具轨迹常使输入长度超出预训练窗口一个数量级，零样本上下文扩展成为开放权重检查点的主流部署路径。现有零样本方法预先固定单一缩放因子：激进因子牺牲短上下文保真度，保守因子在长上下文失效。需要一种方法在短上下文保持基座模型性能，长上下文又能干净外推，且无需微调。

Method: 提出 Jet-Long，一种免调优零样本方法。核心是双焦点构造：1) 局部 RoPE 忠实窗口，保持短程位置编码不变；2) 长程窗口，其缩放因子随当前序列长度动态自适应调整。通过包含-排除注意力融合与即时 RoPE 修正旋转将两窗口无缝合并，推理时基本零开销；并将融合逻辑融入单个 CuTe 内核，实现高效预填充与生成。

Result: 在 Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B（上下文 128K）上：RULER 基准分别比最强基线高 +4.79/+2.18/+2.03 个百分点；HELMET-RAG（被 HELMET 认定为下游长上下文性能最有效预测器）总体准确率最优；PG-19 困惑度最低。推理效率：H100 上长上下文预填充吞吐达 FA2 的 1.39 倍（逼近仅 Hopper 的 FA4），单批生成每长度开销 ≤4%。泛化到混合注意力架构（如 Jet-Nemotron）可进一步提升长上下文性能且无需重训练，超参数稳健易部署。

Conclusion: Jet-Long 通过动态自适应双焦点机制，在零样本设定下同时解决了短上下文保真与长上下文外推的矛盾，配合内核级融合实现了极致推理效率，在多规模模型与权威长上下文基准上均取得 SOTA 结果，并展示了对混合注意力架构的良好泛化与超参数鲁棒性，为开放权重 LLM 的长上下文部署提供了实用、高效、免调优的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern LLMs are increasingly deployed in long-context applications such as retrieval-augmented generation, repository-level coding, and agentic workflows whose accumulated reasoning and tool traces routinely push the input an order of magnitude past the pretraining window, making zero-shot context extension the dominant deployment path for open-weight checkpoints. Most existing zero-shot methods fix a single rescaling factor up front, so an aggressive factor sacrifices short-context fidelity while a conservative one breaks down at long contexts. We propose Jet-Long, a tuning-free zero-shot method that pairs a local RoPE-faithful window with a long-range window whose rescaling factor adapts dynamically to the current sequence length, recovering the base model exactly at short inputs while extrapolating cleanly at long ones. An inclusion-exclusion attention merge and an on-the-fly RoPE correction rotation make the bifocal construction essentially free at inference; fused into a single CuTe kernel, long-context prefill reaches up to $1.39\times$ FA2 throughput on H100 (approaching the Hopper-only FA4), and single-batch generation incurs $\le 4\%$ overhead at every length. On Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B up to 128K context, Jet-Long leads RULER by $+4.79$/$+2.18$/$+2.03$~pp over the strongest baseline at 1.7B/4B/8B, achieves the best overall accuracy on HELMET-RAG (a benchmark identified by HELMET as the most efficient predictor of downstream long-context performance) and attains the lowest PG-19 perplexity. Jet-Long also generalizes to hybrid attention architectures such as Jet-Nemotron for further long-context improvement without retraining, and remains hyperparameter-resilient for ease of deployment.

</details>


### [53] [Architecture Generalization with MetaNCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07743)
*Meet Barot,Daniel Berenberg,Sina Khajehabdollahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MetaNCA框架通过学习局部规则实现神经网络权重的自组织生成，利用Weight Transformer和线性注意力机制，仅依靠计算图上的局部交互更新权重，无需反向传播即可生成多种架构（MLP、CNN、ResNet）的权重，并在MNIST和CIFAR-100上达到200万参数规模，展现出对未见架构的强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受生物神经元通过局部突触交互实现高效学习和终身适应启发，旨在解决传统神经网络依赖全局反向传播、难以自适应调整权重的问题，探索基于局部规则的自组织权重生成机制。

Method: 提出Meta Neural Cellular Automata (MetaNCA)框架：1) 设计Weight Transformer作为局部规则网络，采用线性注意力聚合邻近权重和隐藏状态信息；2) 规则网络在计算图上迭代更新任务网络权重，仅使用局部交互；3) 元训练阶段学习通用规则，推理阶段无需反向传播直接生成目标架构权重。

Result: 在MNIST和CIFAR-100上成功生成MLP、CNN、ResNet权重，规模达200万参数；实验证明MetaNCA能泛化到元训练未见的架构，且训练阶段的架构多样性显著增强泛化能力；生成的权重表现出稳定性和抗扰动鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MetaNCA展示了基于局部交互的自组织权重生成新范式，突破了反向传播依赖，实现了跨架构的权重零样本生成，为神经网络自适应构建和终身学习提供了理论与实践基础。

Abstract: Self-organization is an emergent property of life, driven by the collective behavior of individual components acting on local information. Biological neurons, through local interactions transmitted through synapses, are able to learn efficiently and can adapt their connections over an organism's lifespan. Motivated by these desirable properties of adaptability and local interaction, neural cellular automata (NCA) models have been successful at learning morphogenesis solely through local update rules, demonstrating stability over many updates and robustness to perturbations. In this work, we introduce Meta Neural Cellular Automata (MetaNCA), a framework that learns local rules which self-organize the weights of artificial neural networks. A learned rule network iteratively updates the weights of a task network using only local interactions on the computation graph. We propose a novel Weight Transformer architecture for the local rule network, which uses linear attention to aggregate signals from neighboring weights and hidden states. Once trained, the rule network generates task networks of diverse architectures without backpropagation. We show that MetaNCA generates weights for feedforward MLPs, CNNs, and ResNets on MNIST and CIFAR-100, scaling to networks of 2 million parameters. We further show that MetaNCA generalizes to architectures not seen during meta-training, and that architectural diversity in the training phase strengthens this generalization.

</details>


### [54] [LiST: Lipschitz Scaling Training for Robust and Calibrated Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07745)
*Arthur Chiron,Franck Mamalet,Thomas Massena,Thomas Deltort,Mathieu Serrurier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种名为Lipschitz Scaling Training (LiST)的新训练范式，通过迭代调整全局Lipschitz常数，在准确性-鲁棒性帕累托前沿上找到校准最优的工作点，实现了准确性、鲁棒性和校准性的同时优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确性、鲁棒性和校准性是可靠神经网络的三大核心指标，但通常被分别研究。Lipschitz约束模型虽能保证鲁棒性，但手动选择Lipschitz常数L难以平衡准确性-鲁棒性权衡，且其校准特性鲜有探索。

Method: 发现Lipschitz约束与温度缩放（Temperature Scaling）存在理论和经验联系：存在非平凡L*可使网络开箱即用地校准良好，校准可作为选择准确性-鲁棒性帕累托前沿最优工作点的原则性准据。据此设计LiST：通过迭代调整全局Lipschitz常数收敛到该工作点；引入训练损失中的边界参数构建完全校准的帕累托前沿；收敛后可将校准数据重新整合进训练，提升样本效率且不损失校准性。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100和Tiny-ImageNet上验证，LiST在准确性和鲁棒性上均优于或匹配受约束和无约束基线，且开箱即用地保持良好校准。

Conclusion: LiST为同时实现准确性、鲁棒性和校准性提供了原理性且实用的解决方案，通过Lipschitz常数的自适应调节和校准感知的帕累托前沿构建，解决了三者联合优化的长期挑战。

Abstract: While accuracy, robustness, and calibration are all essential for reliable neural networks, they are often studied separately; developing models that satisfy all three simultaneously remains a central challenge. Lipschitz-constrained models guarantee robustness by design, yet the manual selection of the Lipschitz constraint L governs the resulting accuracy-robustness trade-off, and their calibration properties remain largely underexplored. In this work, we highlight a theoretical and empirical link between the enforced Lipschitz constraint and Temperature Scaling, a state-of-the-art calibration method. Specifically, we find that for a given training scheme, there exists a non-trivial value L* that yields an out-of-the-box calibrated network, and that calibration acts as a principled criterion to select a well-defined operating point on the accuracy-robustness Pareto front. Leveraging these insights, we introduce Lipschitz Scaling Training (LiST), a novel training paradigm that iteratively adjusts the global Lipschitz constant to reach this operating point. Through a margin parameter in the training loss, LiST further enables the construction of a fully calibrated Pareto front, allowing users to navigate the accuracy-robustness trade-off while remaining calibrated throughout. At convergence, LiST also enables the reintegration of calibration data into training, improving sample efficiency without sacrificing calibration. We validate LiST on CIFAR-10/100 and Tiny-ImageNet, demonstrating competitive accuracy and robustness against constrained and unconstrained baselines, while remaining calibrated out of the box. Code is available at GitHub.

</details>


### [55] [Selective Left-Shift: Turning Test-Time Compute and Difficulty-based Curation into Training Data for Low-Resource Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07748)
*Didula Samaraweera,Anjana Supun,Srinath Perera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种三阶段流水线，通过解耦语法习得与算法推理，解决了小语言模型在低资源编程语言（如Julia）上的性能瓶颈，显著提升了代码生成能力，且数据和成本大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在高资源语言（Python、Java）上表现强劲，但在低资源编程语言（LRPLs）如Julia上性能急剧下降。改进小语言模型（SLMs）面临三难困境：监督微调（SFT）受限于数据稀缺，推理时扩展部署成本过高，从头进行强化学习收益近乎为零。因此需要一种新方法解决这一三难困境。

Method: 提出三阶段流水线：第一阶段，将推理时计算前置到离线数据合成引擎，利用迭代编译器和测试反馈生成经过验证的训练样本；第二阶段，在合成的验证数据上微调SLM，嵌入强语法先验；第三阶段，应用基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR），以语言无关的输入/输出测试为基础，SFT先验约束探索避免语法错误。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上应用该流水线，MultiPL-E pass@1提升高达+7.6点，Agnostics LiveCodeBench for Julia提升+14.2点，优于SOTA。且仅使用1/3数据量和1/6成本。推广到Ballerina语言（预训练中几乎无表示）达到49.7% MultiPL-E Pass@1。消融实验确认SFT阶段和执行 grounding 奖励对稳定训练均为必要。

Conclusion: 该三阶段流水线有效解决了低资源编程语言代码生成的三难困境，通过离线合成验证数据嵌入语法先验，结合可验证奖励的强化学习，以极低数据和成本实现显著性能提升，并泛化至极低资源语言，为SLMs在LRPLs上的应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models achieve strong code generation for high resource languages like Python and Java but suffer sharp performance drops on Low-Resource Programming Languages~(LRPLs) such as Julia. Improving Small Language Models~(SLMs) for these languages faces a trilemma: Supervised Fine-Tuning~(SFT) is bottlenecked by data scarcity, inference-time scaling is too expensive for deployment, and Reinforcement Learning from scratch yields near zero advantages. We propose a three-phase pipeline that resolves this trilemma by decoupling syntax acquisition from algorithmic reasoning. First, we \emph{left-shift} inference-time compute to an offline data synthesis engine that uses iterative compiler and test feedback to generate verified training examples. Second, we fine-tune an SLM on this synthetic, verified data to embed strong syntactic priors. Third, we apply Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Reward~(RLVR) grounded by language-agnostic Input/Output tests, where the SFT prior constrains exploration away from syntax errors. Applied to Qwen3-8B, our pipeline improves pass@1 by up to +7.6 points on MultiPL-E and +14.2 points on the Agnostics LiveCodeBench for Julia compared to SOTA results. Furthermore, the pipeline only used $\frac{1}{3}$ data and $\frac{1}{6}$ cost over the previous state-of-the-art. We further demonstrate that the pipeline generalizes to Ballerina achieving 49.7\% MultiPL-E Pass@1, a language with near-zero pretraining representation. Ablations confirm that both the SFT phase and execution-grounded rewards are necessary for stable training.

</details>


### [56] [A Transdiagnostic Space of Disorder Like Phenotypes in Reinforcement Learning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07753)
*Hari Prasad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于评价引导的 PPO 智能体，通过单一可控旋钮操纵认知评价信号来建模七种心理障碍，实现了剂量-反应关系的单调分级，并发现障碍在情感空间中自组织、治疗反应差异、共病非加性交互，且框架可迁移至三维像素环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通过手工调整奖励函数在强化学习智能体中诱导一两种障碍，缺乏系统性、可控性和可复现性。需要一种基于计算精神病学理论、可剂量控制、多障碍统一建模的框架，以服务于计算精神病学验证平台和情感控制失效模式分析。

Method: 将障碍建模重构为对评价引导 PPO 智能体中认知评价信号的剂量可控操纵。七种障碍（焦虑、躁狂、强迫检查、抑郁、冲动、成瘾、创伤后应激）各对应一个基于计算精神病学理论的单一旋钮。使用预注册测定映射公认范式测量症状。跨 10 个随机种子、4 种控制条件进行 1000+ 次运行，报告 95% 置信区间。

Result: 每种障碍均表现出分级单调的剂量-反应关系，控制条件无法复现。障碍在二维情感空间自组织（躁狂镜像焦虑）。移除旋钮可缓解奖励扭曲型障碍（躁狂、检查、成瘾），但回避型障碍（焦虑、PTSD）需分级暴露课程恢复。双旋钮交互呈非加性，产生可验证的共病预测。三种障碍旋钮（抑郁、成瘾、焦虑）可迁移至 MiniWorld 卷积智能体，跨域测定解离成立。

Conclusion: 评价权重参数化了一个可控的情感表型空间，同一组旋钮既能诱导障碍又能建模治疗。该框架不依赖特定网格世界或 PPO 的评价评论家，具有跨环境泛化能力，为计算精神病学提供了严谨、可扩展的验证平台。

Abstract: Modelling psychological disorders in artificial agents offers both a testbed for computational psychiatry and a lens on the failure modes of affective control. Prior work induces one or two disorders in a reinforcement learning (RL) agent by hand-tuned reward shaping, labels the behaviour post hoc, and reports single runs. We recast disorder modelling as dose-controllable manipulation of cognitive appraisal signals in an appraisal-guided PPO agent, expressing seven disorders (anxiety, mania, obsessive-compulsive checking, depression, impulsivity, addiction, and post-traumatic stress) each as a single knob grounded in a computational psychiatry account, with each symptom measured by a preregistered assay mapped to a recognised paradigm. Across more than a thousand runs (10 seeds, four controls, 95% confidence intervals) every disorder shows a graded, monotone dose-response that no control reproduces. Beyond these induced effects, three findings emerge that were not written into the reward: the disorders self-organise into a two-dimensional affective space in which mania mirrors anxiety; removing a knob remits reward distortion disorders (mania, checking, addiction) but not avoidance disorders (anxiety, PTSD), which instead recover under a graded exposure curriculum; and two simultaneous knobs interact nonadditively, yielding testable comorbidity predictions. Appraisal weights thus parameterise a controllable space of affective phenotypes in which the same knobs that induce a disorder can model its treatment. We also show that three disorder knobs (depression, addiction, anxiety) transfer to a three-dimensional pixel environment (MiniWorld) with a standard convolutional agent and no appraisal critic, with cross-assay dissociation confirmed across both domains, indicating the framework is not specific to grid worlds or to PPO's appraisal critic.

</details>


### [57] [Image classification via a quantum-inspired strategy involving a mixture of experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07754)
*Kumari Jyoti,Rohith Babu,Apoorva D. Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种混合经典-量子框架，利用量子启发的专家混合策略进行图像分类，在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上表现优于单一专家，故障率降低约两倍，且在GPU上开销适中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典卷积神经网络依赖基于扩散的模糊和块级池化进行下采样，作者旨在探索更高效的量子启发策略，结合量子编码、酉变换卷积和稳定子码特征提取，以提升模式识别性能。

Method: 构建混合经典-量子框架：量子部分包括振幅编码图像、局部酉操作卷积、多专家并行处理及量子稳定子码特征提取；经典部分用全连接神经网络联合处理多专家特征进行分类预测。

Result: 在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST基准测试中，联合专家分析优于单一专家，图像分类故障率降低约两倍；量子启发策略在GPU工作站上开销适中，具有实用性。

Conclusion: 该量子启发的专家混合框架为经典卷积网络提供了实用替代方案，且量子部分可在量子处理器上执行，展示了混合量子-经典方法在模式识别中的潜力。

Abstract: Pattern recognition problems arise in a variety of physical image processing situations, and convolutional neural networks are a popular scheme for the required feature extraction and classification tasks. The classical networks use diffusion-based smearing and block-wise pooling to downsample the image data and capture important structural features. In this work, we propose and demonstrate a more efficient quantum-inspired strategy involving a mixture of experts. It is a hybrid classical-quantum framework. The quantum part consists of amplitude encoding of the images, convolution using local unitary operations, multiple experts processing the same image with different parameters, and feature extraction using quantum stabiliser codes. The classical part then jointly processes the features extracted by different experts using a standard fully connected neural network for image class prediction. Using MNIST and Fashion-MNIST datasets as benchmarks, we demonstrate that the joint expert analysis outperforms the individual expert one, as well as reduces the failure rate of image class prediction by around a factor of two. The overhead of our quantum-inspired strategy is only moderate on GPU workstations, which makes our proposal a practical alternative to existing classical schemes. We also point out how the quantum part of our framework can be executed on a quantum processor.

</details>


### [58] [The Importance of Encoder Choice:A Tabular-Image Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07756)
*Ilia Koloiarov,Diego Coello de Portugal Mecke,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi,Tom Hanika,Lars Schmidt-Thieme*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次评估最先进表格模型作为图像-表格多模态学习的编码器，解决了上下文学习模型依赖标签的嵌入问题，强调编码器选择在多模态学习中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态学习通常为每种模态配备专用编码器，但表格模态长期以来仅使用简单的MLP作为编码器，而表格领域仍是深度学习的'最后一座未攻克的堡垒'，表明普通MLP编码器不够强。本研究旨在首次评估最先进的表格模型作为图像-表格多模态设置中的编码器。

Method: 研究识别出一个关键障碍：表格领域表现最好的上下文学习模型需要标签来处理实例，导致难以以相同方式嵌入训练和测试实例。作者跨多个此类模型解决了这一问题。

Result: 通过解决上下文学习模型的标签依赖问题，成功将多个最先进的表格模型应用为图像-表格多模态学习的编码器，实现了训练和测试实例的一致嵌入。

Conclusion: 研究强调了编码器因素在多模态学习中的重要性，表明选择强有力的表格编码器能显著提升多模态学习性能。

Abstract: Multimodal learning usually requires a dedicated encoder per modality. When a tabular modality is involved, prior work has been mostly using a \emph{plain MLP} as the encoder. Yet if it were a strong encoder, the tabular domain would not be ``the last unconquered castle for deep learning''. This study evaluates state-of-the-art tabular models as encoders in the image-tabular setting for the first time. An obstacle stands out. In-Context Learning models, among the best performing methods in the tabular domain, require labels to process instances, making it non-trivial to embed training and test instances the same way. We addressed this problem across multiple models of this family. With this study, we would like to highlight the importance of encoder factor in the multimodal learning.

</details>


### [59] [Scalable and Trustworthy Earth Observation Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07758)
*Syed Usama Imtiaz,Mitra Nasr Azadani,Nasrin Alamdari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本章系统综述了遥感基础模型（RSFMs）的设计原则，指出地球观测数据受测量物理和操作决策约束，需领域特定适配。基准测试显示无单一模型通用最优，评估不一致是主要问题。通过有害藻华预测和环境监测站选择两个案例，展示物理感知掩码和强化学习的域引导原则。主张下一代RSFMs应以模态感知迁移和物理合理表示为评价标准，而非仅看基准精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型已将机器学习从孤立的任务特定开发转向通用预训练模型，地球观测（EO）领域拥有大规模、高重访、多模态的卫星/机载数据档案，但可靠现场标签稀缺，且EO数据受测量物理和操作决策约束，RSFMs若无领域特定适配无法可靠/最优迁移，因此需要系统梳理领域约束下的设计原则。

Method: 采用章节综述方法：首先定义遥感领域的FMs范式，综合当前模型全景、预训练目标、架构设计、下游适配及可信度要求；结合近期基准证据分析模型通用性与评估一致性问题；并通过两个环境监测案例研究——基于物理感知的光谱靶向掩码用于有害藻华预测、强化学习用于自适应环境监测站选择——实证说明域引导原则的实践应用。

Result: 基准测试证据表明：不存在通用最优的地空间基础模型；评估协议不一致阻碍公平比较与可靠部署。案例研究验证了物理感知光谱靶向掩码可提升有害藻华预测性能，强化学习可优化环境监测站选择策略，均体现了域引导原则的有效性。

Conclusion: 下一代RSFMs的评价不应仅依赖基准准确率，而应纳入模态感知迁移能力和物理合理表示作为核心指标，以支撑可信的地球观测决策。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) have transformed machine learning from isolated task-specific model development toward general-purpose models pretrained on broad data and adapted to multiple downstream tasks. Earth observation (EO) is an important domain for this paradigm because satellite and airborne archives are large, high-revisit, and increasingly multimodal, while reliable field labels are often sparse. Remote sensing foundation models (RSFMs) cannot be transferred reliably/optimally without domain-specific adaptation. This is because EO data are governed by measurement physics and operational decision constraints. This chapter reviews the design principles arising from these domain-specific constraints. It first defines the FMs paradigm in remote sensing (RS), then synthesizes the current model landscape, pretraining objectives, architecture designs, downstream adaptation and trustworthiness requirements. The chapter also incorporates recent benchmark evidence showing that no single geospatial foundation model is universally best and that inconsistent evaluation remains a major issue to fair comparison and reliable deployment. In addition, two brief environmental monitoring case studies; physics-informed spectral targeted masking for harmful algal bloom prediction and reinforcement learning for adaptive environmental monitoring station selection to illustrate the FMs domain-guided principles in practice. This chapter posits that next-generation RSFMs should be evaluated not only by benchmark accuracy, but also by modality-aware transfer and physically plausible representations for trustworthy EO decisions.

</details>


### [60] [Trustworthy Machine Learning through the Lens of Combinatorial Optimization: Survey and Research Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07762)
*Thibaut Vidal,Julien Ferry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这是一篇综述论文，探讨组合优化在可信赖机器学习中的应用，涵盖训练和训练后任务，包括可解释模型学习、解释生成、鲁棒性分析、公平性审计、模型压缩、隐私攻击与防护等领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习越来越依赖复杂模型，仅凭经验性能指标难以刻画其行为。在预测、生成、决策等任务中，经验性能相似的模型在透明度、可解释性、鲁棒性、公平性、隐私和可认证性方面可能表现截然不同，需要优化和认证导向的推理框架来支持模型训练、选择、审计和认证。

Method: 回顾并综合组合优化与可信赖机器学习交叉领域的最新进展，涵盖训练和训练后任务，包括可解释模型学习、解释生成、鲁棒性分析、公平性审计、模型压缩、隐私攻击与防护等。

Result: 组合优化公式相较于纯启发式方法（如基于梯度的方法）提供了额外能力，特别是全局保证、形式化证书和显式权衡处理。虽然可扩展性仍是重要挑战，但求解器和混合算法的持续进步表明组合优化在可信赖机器学习系统设计与部署中的作用日益增长。

Conclusion: 组合优化为推理模型差异提供了有用框架，支持从模型训练选择到审计认证的各类任务。随着求解器和混合算法的进步，组合优化在可信赖机器学习系统的设计与部署中将发挥越来越重要的作用。

Abstract: Modern machine learning (ML) increasingly relies on complex models whose behavior is difficult to characterize beyond empirical performance metrics. Across a wide range of tasks, including prediction, generation, and decision-making, models with similar empirical performance can exhibit markedly different properties in terms of their transparency, interpretability, robustness, fairness, privacy, and certifiability. This survey highlights how optimization- and certification-oriented reasoning can provide a useful framework for reasoning about such differences, supporting tasks ranging from model training and selection to auditing and certification. We review and synthesize recent advances at the intersection of combinatorial optimization (CO) and trustworthy ML, covering both training and post-training tasks, including interpretable model learning, explanation generation, robustness analysis, fairness auditing, model compression, and privacy attacks and protections. Across these domains, CO formulations offer additional capabilities over purely heuristic approaches, e.g., gradient-based ones, notably global guarantees, formal certificates, and explicit treatment of trade-offs. While scalability remains an important challenge, continued progress in solvers and hybrid algorithms suggests a growing role for CO in the design and deployment of trustworthy ML systems.

</details>


### [61] [Unlocking Temporal Generalization in Hamiltonian Video Dynamics Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07763)
*Eli Laird,Corey Clark*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出 Hamiltonian Generative Networks (HGN) 在非保守系统中存在时间泛化失效问题，分析了两种失效模式（潜在幅度增长和截断误差累积），提出针对性修复，实现了超出训练分布的稳定动力学预测，并为连续时间视频生成的时间泛化提供策略建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型通常以固定步长训练，无法在可变时间分辨率下预测物理动力学，这限制了层级规划、仿真到现实迁移以及科学/游戏引擎应用。HGN 基于连续时间能量函数，原则上独立于观测帧率，但在受外力驱动、耗散的非保守环境中，其时间泛化能力会失效。

Method: 作者在非保守环境中实验分析 HGN 在超出训练步长下的失效机制：1) 由未约束的动作-力映射导致的潜在幅度增长；2) 由欠分辨积分器导致的全局截断误差累积。针对每种机制设计针对性修复（如约束动作-力映射、改进积分器分辨率），并在多个基准上验证。

Result: 修复后的 HGN 能在远超训练分布的时间分辨率下实现稳定的动力学预测。详细分析给出了多项启用连续时间视频生成时间泛化的策略建议。

Conclusion: 通过识别并修复 HGN 在非保守系统中的两大时间泛化失效模式，本文实现了跨时间尺度的鲁棒动力学预测，为构建具备时间泛化能力的连续时间世界模型提供了实用路径。

Abstract: World models are typically trained to predict discrete-time physical dynamics with a fixed step size baked into the model weights, preventing prediction at variable temporal resolutions. This matters for hierarchical planning, sim-to-real transfer, and scientific or game-engine applications that must query the same dynamics at multiple timescales. Hamiltonian Generative Networks (HGN) offer a principled path forward, grounding predictions in a continuous-time energy function that is, in principle, independent of the observation frame rate. In practice, however, their temporal generalization breaks down in non-conservative settings. We show that in externally forced, dissipative environments, HGN rollouts at step sizes beyond the training regime fail due to distinct failure modes, including latent magnitude growth driven by an unconstrained action-force map, and global truncation error accumulation from an under-resolved integrator. We identify a targeted fix for each mechanism and demonstrate stable dynamics prediction at temporal resolutions well outside the training distribution. In a detailed analysis, we recommend several strategies for enabling temporal generalization in continuous-time video generation.

</details>


### [62] [Principled Analysis of Deep Reinforcement Learning Evaluation and Design Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07769)
*Ezgi Korkmaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了强化学习研究中的核心进展因素，揭示了经典评估与设计范式的局限性，建立了强化学习缩放定律的理论基础，并通过大规模实验证明在经典范式下得出的结论可能是错误的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度强化学习在过去十年取得显著进展，但缺乏对缩放规律、算法容量和复杂度的系统性理论分析，且现有的经典评估与设计范式可能导致误导性结论。

Method: 引入强化学习缩放定律的理论框架，分析渐近性能与数据规模的非单调关系，并开展大规模实证实验验证经典范式下的结论偏差。

Result: 发现强化学习算法的渐近性能排名随数据规模变化而非单调变化；在经典设计与评估范式下得出的研究结论存在系统性错误。

Conclusion: 强化学习的缩放、容量与复杂性需要重新审视，现有评估范式需改进以避免误导性结论，为未来深度强化学习研究提供了核心理论指导。

Abstract: Starting from the utilization of deep neural networks to approximate the state-action value function that led to winning one of the most challenging games, to algorithmic advancements that allowed solving problems without even explicitly stating the rules of the challenge at hand, reinforcement learning research has been the center of remarkable scientific progress for the past decade. In this paper, we focus on the key ingredients of this research progress and we analyze the canonical evaluation and design paradigms in reinforcement learning. We introduce the theoretical foundations of scaling laws in reinforcement learning and show that the asymptotic performance of reinforcement learning algorithms does not have a monotone relationship between performance rankings and data-regimes. We conduct large-scale experiments and our results demonstrate that a line of reinforcement learning research under the canonical design and evaluation paradigms resulted in incorrect conclusions. Our analysis and results provide a core analysis on scaling, capacity and complexity of deep reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [63] [Graph-Regularized Deep Learning for EEG-Based Emotion Recognition with Psychologically-Grounded Label Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07773)
*Dongyang Kuang,Zizheng Ma,Yushan Zhang,Xiaocong Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于图正则化的EEG情感识别框架，将情感类别建模为图节点并利用维度情感理论定义边，引入三种互补正则化策略（图标签平滑、图拉普拉斯通勤距离、切片Wasserstein距离）约束预测符合情感拓扑。在三种主干网络和SEED-IV/SEED-V数据集上验证，最高提升5.42%准确率并减少39%心理学上不合理的误分类，展现架构无关的性能增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法将情感类别视为孤立标签，忽略了心理学上情感间的内在关联（如效价-唤醒维度的拓扑结构），导致模型预测可能偏离心理学合理的情感关系，限制了性能上界。

Method: 构建情感图：节点为情感类别，边依据维度情感理论（如环形模型）编码邻近性。提出三种递进复杂度的图正则化：1) 图标签平滑（GLS）：基于图邻接矩阵生成软标签；2) 图拉普拉斯通勤距离：利用谱图理论计算节点间通勤时间作为正则项；3) 切片Wasserstein距离：基于最优传输在图上测量预测分布与目标拓扑的差异。将正则项加入分类损失，兼容AudioTransformer、Conformer、DCGNN等主干。

Result: 在SEED-IV（4类）和SEED-V（5类）数据集上，三种主干均获一致提升：最佳情况准确率提升5.42%，心理学上不合理的误分类减少39%。正则化策略按复杂度递增，效果呈正相关。

Conclusion: 图正则化框架有效融入情感拓扑先验，以架构无关方式提升EEG情感识别性能上界，代码将开源。

Abstract: EEG-based emotion recognition is critical for mental health monitoring and affective brain-computer interfaces, yet existing deep learning approaches often treat emotion classes as isolated labels, ignoring their psychological interdependencies. We propose a graph-regularized learning framework that conceptualizes emotions as nodes in a graph where edges encode proximity based on dimensional emotion theories. We adapt three complementary regularization strategies--Graph Label Smoothing (intuitive soft labeling), Commuting distance on graph via Graph Laplacian (spectral graph theory), and Sliced Wasserstein Distance (optimal transport on graph)--ordered by increasing computational complexity. These strategies penalize model predictions that deviate from the established emotion topology. Our framework is evaluated across three representative backbone architectures: AudioTransformer (pure transformer), Conformer (CNN-transformer hybrid), and DCGNN (causal graph neural network), demonstrating architecture-agnostic benefits. Experiments on SEED-IV (4 classes) and SEED-V (5 classes) datasets show consistent improvements: best case up to +5.42% accuracy and 39% reduction in psychologically implausible misclassifications. Ultimately, our framework help raise the upper bound of performance achievable with standard approaches. Code will be released.

</details>


### [64] [A law of robustness for two-layer neural networks with arbitrary weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07778)
*Yitzchak Shmalo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了 Bubeck, Li 和 Nagaraj 的猜想，即对于通用数据，任意双层神经网络拟合 n 个带噪标签的 Lipschitz 常数至少为 sqrt(n/m) 阶，仅相差一个对数因子，且无需权重大小限制。适用于所有连续分段线性激活函数（包括 ReLU），通过函数空间覆盖和刚性引理实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bubeck, Li 和 Nagaraj 猜想双层神经网络拟合带噪数据的 Lipschitz 常数下界为 sqrt(n/m)，但 Bubeck 和 Sellke 的证明需要参数有界。双层无界权重情况需要新论证。本文旨在去除权重有界假设，证明猜想在连续分段线性激活下的普适性。

Method: 采用函数空间覆盖替代参数空间覆盖，核心是刚性引理：在单位球和 d>=3 的球面上，每个典型折点的系数由实现函数的 Lipschitz 常数控制，因为不同超平面上的折点在一般点无法相互抵消。利用此引理建立 Lipschitz 常数与折点数量的关系。

Result: 对于从 S^{d-1} (d>=3) 或 N(0,I_d/d) 均匀采样的数据，标签在 [-1,1] 且噪声水平 σ^2>0，任意宽度 m 的双层网络（任意实数权重、偏置、仿射跳跃连接）若拟合误差 ε 低于噪声底，则 Lip(f) >= c ε sqrt(n/((K-1)m+1) log(C (K-1)m+1 n d/ε))，高概率成立。同时给出基于实现折点数 k(f) 的版本。d=2 时刚性失效，存在宽度 2n 的 ReLU 网络以 O(1) Lipschitz 常数插值，符合过参数化端点的下界。

Conclusion: 论文解决了双层无界权重神经网络的 Lipschitz 下界猜想，证明了除对数因子外的最优下界，揭示了函数空间刚性作为核心几何性质，且指出了 d=2 的特殊性。结果对理解神经网络的泛化和优化具有理论意义。

Abstract: Bubeck, Li and Nagaraj conjectured that, for generic data, any two-layer neural network with $m$ neurons that fits $n$ noisy labels must have Lipschitz constant at least of order $\sqrt{n/m}$, with no restriction on the size of the weights. Bubeck and Sellke proved a universal version of this law for Lipschitz-parameterized classes, but under a polynomial bound on the parameters; at depth three that boundedness hypothesis is genuinely necessary. The two-layer unbounded-weight case requires a different argument. We prove the conjectured law, up to one logarithmic factor, for every continuous piecewise-linear activation, in particular for ReLU networks. For data drawn uniformly from $\mathbb{S}^{d-1}$, $d\ge3$, or from $N(0,I_d/d)$, labels in $[-1,1]$ with noise level $σ^2>0$, and any width-$m$ two-layer network with arbitrary real weights, biases and affine skip connection, fitting the data $\varepsilon$ below the noise floor forces $\mathrm{Lip}(f)\ge c\,\varepsilon\sqrt{n/(\bar m\log(C\bar m nd/\varepsilon))}$, $\bar m=(K-1)m+1$, with high probability. A realized-kink-count version holds on the same event: every realized two-layer piecewise-linear function with $k(f)\le n$ distinct kink hyperplanes obeys the bound with $\bar m$ replaced by $k(f)+1$, irrespective of how many redundant hidden units parameterize it. The proof replaces parameter-space covering, impossible for unbounded weights, by a function-space covering. The central deterministic ingredient is a rigidity lemma: on $B_2$, and on $\mathbb{S}^{d-1}$ for $d\ge3$, the coefficient of each canonical kink is controlled by the Lipschitz constant of the realized function, because kinks on distinct hyperplanes cannot cancel at generic points. Rigidity genuinely fails at $d=2$, and an explicit two-layer ReLU interpolant with $O(1)$ Lipschitz constant at width $2n$ matches the law at the overparameterized endpoint.

</details>


### [65] [Predicting Pseudo-nitzschia harmful algal blooms along the Portuguese Coast using satellite-derived predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07834)
*Ayman Bnoussaad,El Khalil Cherif,Ligia Pinto,Ramiro Neves,Alexandra D. Silva,Alexandre Bernardino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一个时空机器学习框架，利用卫星衍生预测因子预测葡萄牙大西洋沿岸假单胞菌属硅藻有害藻华（HAB）发生，集成树模型达到0.77的AUC，证明了卫星支持的HAB早期预警系统的操作可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 假单胞菌属硅藻在葡萄牙大西洋沿岸反复引发有害藻华，威胁沿海生态系统和贝类养殖业，亟需基于卫星数据的现实预报约束下的早期预警系统。

Method: 利用5,882个观测数据刻画贝类生产区（L1-L9）的环境生物变异性；针对热点区L1-L2，使用十年数据集（2013-2023，1,440观测）及1000+卫星预测因子（海表温度、上升流指数、叶绿素a、浮游植物功能类型等）；采用河流感知空间聚类划分生态亚区；实施严格时空交叉验证（同时保留整年份和空间簇）防止泄漏，模拟真实预报条件。

Result: HAB发生在各模型类别中均表现出中等可预测性。集成树模型表现最优：随机森林仅用环境预测因子AUC为0.74±0.05；极端树加入生物变量后AUC达0.77±0.06。特征重要性分析显示季节结构、空间背景和滞后环境条件主导模型决策，生物指标在物理条件适宜期细化爆发可能性。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了面向东部边界上升流海岸的卫星支持HAB早期预警系统的操作相关技能。

Abstract: Pseudo-nitzschia diatoms pose recurrent risks to coastal ecosystems and shellfish harvesting along the Portuguese Atlantic coast. Here we develop and evaluate a spatio-temporal machine-learning framework to predict harmful algal bloom (HAB) occurrence using exclusively satellite-derived predictors under realistic forecasting constraints. We characterised environmental and biological variability across shellfish production zones (L1-L9) using 5,882 observations, providing system-wide context. Predictive models were developed for zones L1-L2, a hotspot for Pseudo-nitzschia and domoic acid events, using a decade-long dataset (2013-2023; 1,440 observations; more than 1,000 satellite-based predictors including sea surface temperature, an upwelling index, chlorophyll-a, and plankton functional types). Sampling locations were partitioned into ecologically meaningful sub-regions using a river-aware spatial clustering scheme. A stringent spatio-temporal cross-validation strategy that simultaneously withholds entire years and spatial clusters prevents leakage and closely mimics real-world forecasting conditions. HAB occurrence proved moderately predictable across model classes and feature configurations. Ensemble tree-based methods achieved the strongest discrimination: Random Forest reached 0.74 +/- 0.05 with environmental predictors; Extra Trees reached 0.77 +/- 0.06 with biological variables added. Feature-importance analyses revealed that seasonal structure, spatial context, and lagged environmental conditions dominate model decisions, while biological indicators refine bloom likelihood within physically favourable periods. The framework demonstrates operationally relevant skill for satellite-supported HAB early-warning systems along eastern boundary upwelling coasts.

</details>


### [66] [Explaining Near-Zero Hessian Eigenvalues Through Approximate Symmetries in Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07845)
*Marcel Kühn,Bernd Rosenow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了神经网络训练损失海森矩阵谱中大量近零特征值的起源：它们源于网络参数化的连续对称性被弱打破后产生的伪戈德斯通模式。在深度线性网络中这些对称性产生精确的零模式；引入ReLU非线性后，对称性被弱显式打破，导致海森矩阵的大部分谱（本征值）落在对称性子空间内，而高曲率方向正交于该子空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已解释海森矩阵最大特征值的来源，但海量极小特征值（谱的主体）的起源仍不清楚。作者旨在将海森矩阵主体与网络参数化的连续对称性弱打破联系起来，阐明近零模式的物理机制。

Method: 1. 理论分析深度线性网络，显式构造由连续对称性生成的平坦方向及其精确零模式本征向量。2. 将ReLU非线性视为微扰，分析其如何弱显式打破对称性。3. 在本征向量层面解析海森矩阵谱，区分高曲率方向与对称性子空间。4. 在双层ReLU学生-教师模型和CIFAR-10训练的网络中验证机制，并扩展到卷积层。

Result: 深度线性网络中连续对称性产生精确零模式；ReLU弱打破对称性，导致海森矩阵主体谱几乎完全位于对称性子空间内，高曲率方向正交于该子空间。实验在学生-教师模型和CIFAR-10上均验证了这一机制，卷积网络也表现出相同诊断特征。
